n_{1}\leq N+2n_{\beta}+2\end{subarray}}E_{\mathbf{N}}[\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}T^{n_{2}-1-n_{\beta}}\widehat{\phi}_{0}]+\Phi_{0}^{2}\Bigg]+C\Phi_{0}^{2}(1+\tau)^{-4-2\beta_{0}-2N+2\delta}.\end{split}

To estimate the
\phi_{\geq 1}
part of
\phi
, we consider separately the following three cases: 1)
\beta_{0}<2n_{\beta}
and
\beta_{1}>2n_{\beta}
, 2)
\beta_{0}<2n_{\beta}
and
\beta_{1}\leq 2n_{\beta}
, 3)
\beta_{0}\geq 2n_{\beta}
and
\beta_{1}>2n_{\beta}
. Since
\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1}=\phi_{\geq 1}
, we can omit the terms above involving
\Phi_{0}^{2}
when restricting to
\phi_{\geq 1}
.

Case 1):

We apply the estimates in
(ii)
of Proposition
10.7
with
p=2n_{\beta}-\beta_{0}+\delta
,
N
replaced by
N+n_{\beta}
and
\delta>0
appropriately small. Note that this choice of
p
lies in the allowed range of
p
in
(ii)
of Proposition
10.7
, since
\beta_{0}>2n_{\beta}-1
and
\beta_{1}>2n_{\beta}-1
. We then obtain:

\begin{split}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}}&r^{-\beta_{0}+\delta}(T^{N}\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1})^{2}+r^{2-\beta_{0}+\delta}(XT^{N}\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1})^{2}\,d\sigma dr\\
\leq&\>C(1+\tau)^{-3-\beta_{0}-2n_{\beta}+\max\{2n_{\beta}+1-\beta_{1},0\}+2\delta-2N}\\
\cdot&\Bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq n_{1}+n_{2}+n_{3}\leq 2(N+2n_{\beta}+1)\\
n_{1}+n_{2}\leq N+2n_{\beta}+1\end{subarray}}\int_{\Sigma_{0}\cap\{r\geq R\}}r^{\min\{\beta_{1}-2n_{\beta},1\}-\delta}|\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}L(rL)^{n_{2}}T^{n_{3}-1-n_{\beta}}\widehat{\psi}_{\geq 1}|^{2}\,d\sigma dr\\
&+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq n_{1}+n_{2}\leq 3(N+2n_{\beta}+1)\\
n_{1}\leq 2n_{\beta}+1\end{subarray}}E_{\mathbf{N}}[\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}T^{n_{2}-1-n_{\beta}}\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1}]\Bigg].\end{split}

Case 2):

We apply the estimates in
(iii)
of Proposition
10.7
with
p=2n_{\beta}-\beta_{0}+\delta
,
N
replaced by
N+n_{\beta}
and
\delta>0
appropriately small. Note that this choice of
p
lies in the allowed range of
p
in
(ii)
of Proposition
10.7
, since
\beta_{0}>2n_{\beta}-1
and
\beta_{1}>2n_{\beta}-1
by assumption. We obtain:

\begin{split}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}}&r^{-\beta_{0}+\delta}(T^{N}\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1})^{2}+r^{2-\beta_{0}+\delta}(XT^{N}\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1})^{2}\,d\sigma dr\\
\leq&\>C(1+\tau)^{-2-\beta_{0}-\beta_{1}+2\delta}\\
\cdot&\Bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq n_{1}+n_{2}+n_{3}\leq 2(N+2n_{\beta})\\
n_{1}+n_{2}\leq N+2n_{\beta}\end{subarray}}\int_{\Sigma_{0}\cap\{r\geq R\}}r^{2+\beta_{1}-2n_{\beta}-\delta}|\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}L(rL)^{n_{2}}T^{n_{3}-n_{\beta}}\widehat{\psi}_{\geq 1}|^{2}\,d\sigma dr\\
&+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq n_{1}+n_{2}\leq 3(N+2n_{\beta})\\
n_{1}\leq N+2n_{\beta}+1\end{subarray}}E_{\mathbf{N}}[\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}T^{n_{2}-n_{\beta}}\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1}]\Bigg].\end{split}

Case 3):

We apply the estimates in
(ii)
of Proposition
10.7
with
p=2+2n_{\beta}-\beta_{0}+\delta
,
N
replaced by
N+n_{\beta}+1
and
\delta>0
appropriately small. Note that this choice of
p
lies in the allowed range of
p
in
(ii)
of Proposition
10.7
, since
\beta_{0}>2n_{\beta}
and
\beta_{1}>2n_{\beta}
by assumption. We obtain:

\begin{split}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}}&r^{-\beta_{0}+\delta}(T^{N}\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1})^{2}+r^{2-\beta_{0}+\delta}(XT^{N}\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1})^{2}\,d\sigma dr\\
\leq&\>C(1+\tau)^{-3-\beta_{0}-2n_{\beta}+\max\{2n_{\beta}+1-\beta_{1},0\}+2\delta-2N}\\
\cdot&\Bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq n_{1}+n_{2}+n_{3}\leq 2(N+2n_{\beta}+2)\\
n_{1}+n_{2}\leq N+2n_{\beta}+2\end{subarray}}\int_{\Sigma_{0}\cap\{r\geq R\}}r^{\min\{\beta_{1}-2n_{\beta},1\}-\delta}|\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}L(rL)^{n_{2}}T^{n_{3}-1-n_{\beta}}\widehat{\psi}_{\geq 1}|^{2}\,d\sigma dr\\
&+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq n_{1}+n_{2}\leq 3(N+2n_{\beta}+2)\\
n_{1}\leq 2n_{\beta}+1\end{subarray}}E_{\mathbf{N}}[\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}T^{n_{2}-1-n_{\beta}}\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1}]\Bigg].\end{split}

We conclude that
(ii)
must hold.

Consider now
\beta_{0}<1
and restrict first to
\phi_{0}
. By (
9.3
) with
\ell=0
and a suitably choice of
q
, it follows that for
\delta>0
arbitrarily small

\begin{split}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}}r^{-1+2\beta_{0}}(X(w_{0}^{-1}\widehat{\phi}_{0}))^{2}\,d\sigma dr\leq&\>C\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}}r^{-2+\beta_{0}}(\mathcal{L}\widehat{\phi}_{0})^{2}\,d\sigma dr\\
\leq&\>C\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}}r^{\beta_{0}}(XT\widehat{\psi}_{0})^{2}+r^{-4+\beta_{0}}(T\widehat{\phi}_{0})^{2}+r^{-2+\beta_{0}}(r^{2}\tilde{h}h)^{2}(T^{2}\widehat{\phi}_{0})^{2}+r^{2+\beta_{0}}(\square_{g}\Phi|_{\Sigma_{\tau}})^{2}\,d\sigma dr\\
\leq&\>CE_{\mathbf{N}}[T\widehat{\phi}_{0}](\tau)+C\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}}r^{\beta_{0}}(XT\widehat{\psi}_{0})^{2}+r^{2}(\square_{g}\Phi|_{\Sigma_{\tau}})^{2}\,d\sigma dr.\end{split}

Hence, we can apply Proposition
10.6
with
p=\beta_{0}
and
N
replaced with
N+1
, together with the above estimate applied to
T^{N}\phi_{0}
to obtain

\begin{split}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}}&r^{-1+2\beta_{0}}(XT^{N}w_{0}^{-1}\widehat{\phi}_{0})^{2}\,d\sigma dr\\
\leq&\>C(1+\tau)^{\max\{1-3\beta_{0},0\}+\beta_{0}-5-2N+\delta}\Bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq n_{1}+n_{2}+n_{3}\leq 2(N+2)\\
n_{1}+n_{2}\leq N+2\end{subarray}}\int_{\Sigma_{0}\cap\{r\geq R\}}r^{\min\{3\beta_{0},1\}-\delta}|\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}L(rL)^{n_{2}}T^{n_{3}-1}\widehat{\psi}_{0}|^{2}\,d\sigma dr\\
&+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq n_{1}+n_{2}\leq 3(N+2)\\
n_{1}\leq N+2\end{subarray}}E_{\mathbf{N}}[\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}T^{n_{2}-1}\widehat{\phi}]+\Phi_{0}^{2}\Bigg]+C|\Phi_{0}|^{2}(1+\tau)^{-4-2\beta_{0}-2N+\delta}.\end{split}

In order to obtain an estimate for
\phi_{\geq 1}
, we instead start as in the
\beta_{0}>1
case, using that
\phi_{\geq 1}=\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1}
, to obtain:

\begin{split}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}}r^{-\beta_{0}+\delta}\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1}^{2}+r^{2-\beta_{0}+\delta}(X\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1})^{2}\,d\sigma dr\leq&\>C\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}}r^{2-\beta_{0}+\delta}(XT\widehat{\psi}_{\geq 1})^{2}+CE_{\mathbf{N}}[T\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1}](\tau).\end{split}

Then we apply Proposition
10.6
with
p=2-\beta_{0}+\delta
and
\phi_{\geq 1}
replaced by
T^{N}\phi_{\geq 1}
to obtain:

\begin{split}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}}&r^{-\beta_{0}+\delta}(T^{N}\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1})^{2}+r^{2-\beta_{0}+\delta}(XT^{N}\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1})^{2}\,d\sigma dr\\
\leq&\>C(1+\tau)^{-3-\beta_{0}-2N+2\delta}\Bigg[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq n_{1}+n_{2}+n_{3}\leq 2(N+2)\\
n_{1}+n_{2}\leq N+2\end{subarray}}\int_{\Sigma_{0}\cap\{r\geq R\}}r|\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}L(rL)^{n_{2}}T^{n_{3}-1-n_{\beta}}\widehat{\psi}_{\geq 1}|^{2}\,d\sigma dr\\
&+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq n_{1}+n_{2}\leq 3(N+2)\\
n_{1}\leq N+2\end{subarray}}E_{\mathbf{N}}[\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}T^{n_{2}-1-n_{\beta}}\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1}]\Bigg].\end{split}

By combining the above estimates, we conclude
(i)
.
∎

Since
\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{0},N,\delta}^{(1)}[\phi]
and
\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{0},N}^{(n_{\beta}+1)}[\phi]
are expressed in terms of time integrals of
\phi
along
\Sigma_{0}
, it remains to show that they can be bounded in terms of initial data for
\phi
itself.

Proposition 10.9
.

There exists a constant
C=C(M,V_{\alpha},h,N,\delta)>0
, such that

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{0},N,\delta}^{(1)}[\phi]\leq

\displaystyle\>C\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{0},N,\delta}[\phi]\hskip 10.00002pt(\beta_{0}<1),

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{0},N,\delta}^{(n_{\beta}+1)}[\phi]\leq

\displaystyle\>C\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{0},N,\delta}[\phi]\hskip 10.00002pt(\beta_{0}>1),

with

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{0},N,\delta}[\phi]:=

\displaystyle\>\sum_{n\leq N+2}\left(||((r+1)^{2}(rX)^{n}X(r\phi_{0})||_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma_{0})}^{2}+||(rX)^{n}\phi_{0}||_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma_{0})}^{2}+||r(rX)^{n}T\phi_{0}||_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma_{0})}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n_{1}+n_{2}\leq N+2}\int_{\Sigma_{0}\cap\{r\geq R\}}r^{3-\delta}|\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}X(rX)^{n_{2}}\widehat{\psi}_{\geq 1}|^{2}+r^{-1-\delta}|\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}(rX)^{n_{2}}T\widehat{\psi}_{\geq 1}|^{2}\,d\sigma dr

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq n_{1}+n_{2}+n_{3}\leq 2N+3\\
n_{1}+n_{2}\leq N+2\end{subarray}}\int_{\Sigma_{0}\cap\{r\geq R\}}r^{\min\{3\beta_{0},1\}-\delta}|L(rL)^{n_{2}}T^{n_{3}}\widehat{\psi}_{0}|^{2}+r^{1-\delta}|\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}L(rL)^{n_{2}}T^{n_{3}-1}{\psi}_{\geq 1}|^{2}\,d\sigma dr

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq n_{1}+n_{2}\leq 3N+5\\
n_{1}\leq N+2\end{subarray}}E_{\mathbf{N}}[\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}T^{n_{2}}\widehat{\phi}]+\mathfrak{I}_{\beta}^{2}[\phi]\hskip 20.00003pt(\beta_{0}<1),

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{0},N,\delta}[\phi]:=

\displaystyle\>\sum_{n\leq N+3n_{\beta}+2}\Biggl(||(r+1)^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\beta_{0}}{2}}(rX)^{n}X(r\phi_{0})||_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma_{0})}^{2}

\displaystyle+M^{4}||r^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\beta_{0}}{2}}(rX)^{n}\phi_{0}||_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma_{0})}^{2}+M^{4}||r^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\beta_{0}}{2}}(rX)^{n}T\phi_{0}||_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma_{0})}^{2}\Biggr)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n_{1}+n_{2}\leq N+2n_{\beta}+2}\int_{\Sigma_{0}\cap\{r\geq R\}}r^{2+\min\{2n_{\beta}+1,\beta_{1}\}-\delta}|\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}X(rX)^{n_{2}}\widehat{\psi}_{\geq 1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+M^{4}r^{-2+\min\{2n_{\beta}+1,\beta_{1}\}-\delta}|\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}X(rX)^{n_{2}}T\widehat{\psi}_{\geq 1}|^{2}\,d\sigma dr

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq n_{1}+n_{2}+n_{3}\leq 2N+3n_{\beta}+5\\
n_{1}+n_{2}\leq N+2n_{\beta}+2\end{subarray}}\int_{\Sigma_{0}\cap\{r\geq R\}}r|\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}L(rL)^{n_{2}}T^{n_{3}}\widehat{\psi}_{0}|^{2}

\displaystyle+r^{\min\{\beta_{1}-2n_{\beta},1\}-\delta}|\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}L(rL)^{n_{2}}T^{n_{3}}{\psi}_{\geq 1}|^{2}+r^{2+\min\{\beta_{1}-2n_{\beta},0\}-\delta}|\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}L(rL)^{n_{2}}T^{n_{3}}{\psi}_{\geq 1}|^{2}\,d\sigma dr

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq n_{1}+n_{2}\leq 3N+5n_{\beta}+5\\
n_{1}\leq N+2n_{\beta}+2\end{subarray}}E_{\mathbf{N}}[\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}T^{n_{2}}\widehat{\phi}]+\mathfrak{I}_{\beta}^{2}[\phi]\hskip 20.00003pt(\beta_{0}>1).

Proof.

We first consider the case
\beta_{0}<1
and the norm
\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{0},N,\delta}^{(1)}[\phi]
. Note that all the terms in
\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{0},N,\delta}^{(1)}[\phi]
with
n_{3}\geq 1
can directly be expressed in terms of
\phi
along
\Sigma_{0}
. It therefore remains to estimate the terms with
n_{3}=0
. Furthermore, without loss of generality, we can replace
L
derivatives with
X
derivatives in
\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{0},N,\delta}^{(1)}[\phi]
, using the relation (
2.2
).

By
(i)
of Corollary
10.2
, we can estimate

\begin{split}E_{\mathbf{N}}[T^{-1}\widehat{\phi}_{0}]+&\sum_{n\leq N+2}\int_{\Sigma_{0}\cap\{r\geq R\}}r^{\min\{3\beta_{0},1\}-\delta}|X(rX)^{n}T^{-1}\widehat{\psi}_{0}|^{2}\,d\sigma dr\\
\ \leq&\>C\sum_{n\leq N+2}\left(||((r+1)^{2}(rX)^{n}X(r\phi_{0})||_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma_{0})}^{2}+||(rX)^{n}\phi_{0}||_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma_{0})}^{2}+||r(rX)^{n}T\phi_{0}||_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma_{0})}^{2}\right).\end{split}

Furthermore, we can apply Proposition
9.9
to estimate:

\begin{split}\sum_{n_{1}\leq N+2}E_{\mathbf{N}}[\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}T^{-1}\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1}]+&\sum_{n_{1}+n_{2}\leq N+2}\int_{\Sigma_{0}\cap\{r\geq R\}}r^{1-\delta}|\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}X(rX)^{n_{2}}T^{-1}\widehat{\psi}_{\geq 1}|^{2}\,d\sigma dr\\
\ \leq&\>CE_{\mathbf{N}}[\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1}]+CE_{\mathbf{N}}[T\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1}]\\
&+\sum_{n_{1}+n_{2}\leq N+2}\int_{\Sigma_{0}\cap\{r\geq R\}}r^{3-\delta}|\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}X(rX)^{n_{2}}\widehat{\psi}_{\geq 1}|^{2}+r^{-1-\delta}|\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}(rX)^{n_{2}}T\widehat{\psi}_{\geq 1}|^{2}\,d\sigma dr.\end{split}

Now we consider the case
\beta_{0}>1
and the norm
\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{0},N,\delta}^{(n_{\beta}+1)}[\phi]
. Note that all the terms in
\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{0},N,\delta}^{(n_{\beta}+1)}[\phi]
with
n_{3}\geq 1+n_{\beta}
can directly be expressed in terms of
\phi
along
\Sigma_{0}
. It therefore remains to estimate the terms with
n_{3}\leq n_{\beta}
. Furthermore, without loss of generality, we can replace
L
derivatives with
X
derivatives in
\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{0},N,\delta}^{(n_{\beta}+1)}[\phi]
, using the relation (
2.2
).

By
(ii)
of Corollary
10.2
, we can estimate

\begin{split}E_{\mathbf{N}}[T^{-1-n_{\beta}}\widehat{\phi}_{0}]+&\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n_{2}\leq N+2n_{\beta}+2\\
n_{3}\leq n_{\beta}\end{subarray}}\int_{\Sigma_{0}\cap\{r\geq R\}}r|X(rX)^{n_{2}}T^{n_{3}-1-n_{\beta}}\widehat{\psi}_{0}|^{2}\,d\sigma dr\\
\leq&\>C\sum_{n\leq N+3n_{\beta}+2}\Biggl(||(r+1)^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\beta_{0}}{2}}(rX)^{n}X(r\phi_{0})||_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma_{0})}\\
&+||r^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\beta_{0}}{2}}(rX)^{n}\phi_{0}||_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma_{0})}+||r^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\beta_{0}}{2}}(rX)^{n}T\phi_{0}||_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma_{0})}\Biggr).\end{split}

In order to estimate the terms involving
\phi_{\geq 1}
, it is convenient to split the cases
\beta_{1}>2n_{\beta}
and
\beta_{0}\leq 2n_{\beta}
. Suppose
\beta_{1}>2n_{\beta}
. Then we can control via Corollary
9.10
:

\begin{split}\sum_{n_{1}+n_{2}\leq N+2n_{\beta}+2}&E_{\mathbf{N}}[\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}T^{-1-n_{\beta}}\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1}]+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n_{1}+n_{2}\leq N+2n_{\beta}+2\\
n_{3}\leq n_{\beta}\end{subarray}}\int_{\Sigma_{0}\cap\{r\geq R\}}r^{\min\{\beta_{1}-2n_{\beta},1\}-\delta}|\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}X(rX)^{n_{2}}T^{n_{3}-1-n_{\beta}}{\psi}_{\geq 1}|^{2}\\
&+r^{2-\delta}|\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}X(rX)^{n_{2}}T^{n_{3}-n_{\beta}}{\psi}_{\geq 1}|^{2}\,d\sigma dr\\
\leq&\>CE_{\mathbf{N}}[\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1}]+CME_{\mathbf{N}}[T\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1}]+C\sum_{n_{1}+n_{2}\leq N+2n_{\beta}+2}\int_{\Sigma_{0}\cap\{r\geq R\}}r^{2+\min\{2n_{\beta}+1,\beta_{1}\}-\delta}|\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}X(rX)^{n_{2}}\widehat{\psi}_{\geq 1}|^{2}\\
&+r^{-2+\min\{2n_{\beta}+1,\beta_{1}\}-\delta}|\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}X(rX)^{n_{2}}T\widehat{\psi}_{\geq 1}|^{2}\,d\sigma dr.\end{split}

Suppose
\beta_{1}\leq 2n_{\beta}
. Then we can similarly control via Corollary
9.10
:

\begin{split}E_{\mathbf{N}}[T^{-1-n_{\beta}}\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1}]+&\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n_{1}+n_{2}\leq N+2n_{\beta}+2\\
n_{3}\leq n_{\beta}\end{subarray}}\int_{\Sigma_{0}\cap\{r\geq R\}}r^{\beta_{1}-2n_{\beta}-\delta}|\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}X(rX)^{n_{2}}T^{n_{3}-1-n_{\beta}}{\psi}_{\geq 1}|^{2}\\
&+r^{2+\beta_{1}-2n_{\beta}-\delta}|\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}X(rX)^{n_{2}}T^{n_{3}-n_{\beta}}{\psi}_{\geq 1}|^{2}\,d\sigma dr\\
\leq&\>CE_{\mathbf{N}}[\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1}]+CME_{\mathbf{N}}[T\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1}]+C\sum_{n_{1}+n_{2}\leq N+2n_{\beta}+2}\int_{\Sigma_{0}\cap\{r\geq R\}}r^{2+\beta_{1}-\delta}|\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}X(rX)^{n_{2}}\widehat{\psi}_{\geq 1}|^{2}\\
&+r^{-2+\beta_{1}-\delta}|\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{n_{1}}X(rX)^{n_{2}}T\widehat{\psi}_{\geq 1}|^{2}\,d\sigma dr.\end{split}

We conclude the estimates in the proposition by combining the above.
∎

10.3
L^{\infty}
estimates

In this section, we use the (
r
-weighted) energy decay estimates of Sections
10.1
and
10.2
to obtain
L^{\infty}
estimates along each
\Sigma_{\tau}
for
\widehat{\phi}
. We obtain pointwise estimates from energy estimates by applying the fundamental theorem of calculus in
r
together with Sobolev estimates on
\mathbb{S}^{2}
.

Proposition 10.10
.

Let
\beta_{0}\notin 2\mathbb{N}_{0}+1
and
\delta>0
arbitrarily small. Then there exists a constant
C=C(M,h,V_{\alpha},N,\delta)>0
such that for all
0\leq k\leq n_{\beta}+1
:

\displaystyle||T^{N-k}\phi-\Phi_{0}w_{0}T^{N-k}((1+\tau+2r)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{0}}(1+\tau)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{0}})||_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma_{\tau})}

(10.27)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\>Cr^{-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\beta_{0}}(1+\tau+2r)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{0}}(1+\tau)^{-1+\frac{1}{2}\max\{1-3\beta_{0},0\}+k-N+\delta}\sqrt{\sum_{0\leq k\leq 2}\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{0},N,\delta}[\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{k}\phi]},\hskip 10.00002pt(\beta_{0}<1)

\displaystyle||T^{N-k}\phi-\Phi_{0}w_{0}T^{N-k}((1+\tau+2r)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{0}}(1+\tau)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{0}})||_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma_{\tau})}

(10.28)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\>Cr^{-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\beta_{0}}(1+\tau+2r)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{0}}(1+\tau)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{0}-\frac{1}{2}\min\{1-\beta_{0}+2n_{\beta},\beta_{1}-\beta_{0}\}-N+\delta}\sqrt{\sum_{0\leq k\leq 2}\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{0},N,\delta}[\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{k}\phi]}.\hskip 10.00002pt(\beta_{0}>1)

Proof.

We consider first
\widehat{\phi}
. By integrating
X\widehat{\phi}
, using that
\phi(\tau,r,\theta,\varphi)\to 0
and
\Phi(\tau,r,\theta,\varphi)\to 0
as
r\to\infty
, we can estimate for all
r_{0}\geq 2M
.

\begin{split}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}\cap\{r=r_{0}\}}\widehat{\phi}^{2}\,d\sigma=&\>\left(\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}\cap\{r\geq r_{0}\}}X\widehat{\phi}\,d\sigma dr\right)^{2}\\
\leq&\>\int_{r_{0}}^{\infty}r^{-2}\,dr\cdot\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}\cap\{r\geq r_{0}\}}r^{2}(X\phi)^{2}\,d\sigma dr\\
\leq&\>r^{-\frac{1}{2}}E_{\mathbf{N}}[\phi](\tau).\end{split}

We now integrate
X(\widehat{\psi}^{2})
in
R\leq r\leq r_{0}
to obtain additionally:

\begin{split}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}\cap\{r=r_{0}\}}\widehat{\psi}^{2}\,d\sigma=&\>\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}\cap\{R\leq r\geq r_{0}\}}X(\widehat{\psi}^{2})\,d\sigma dr+\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}\cap\{r=R\}}\widehat{\psi}^{2}\,d\sigma\\
\leq&\>C\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}\cap\{R\leq r\geq r_{0}\}}r^{-1+\delta}\widehat{\psi}^{2}+r^{1+\delta}(X\widehat{\psi})^{2}\,d\sigma dr+CE_{\mathbf{N}}[\phi](\tau)\\
\leq&\>C\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}\cap\{R\leq r\geq r_{0}\}}r^{1+\delta}(L\widehat{\psi})^{2}\,d\sigma dr+CE_{\mathbf{N}}[\phi](\tau),\end{split}

where we applied (
2.10
) to absorb the
r^{-1+\delta}\widehat{\psi}^{2}
term in the last step.

Hence, we apply the energy estimates in Propositions
10.5
,
10.6
and
10.7
together with Proposition
10.9
to obtain for all
r_{0}\geq 2M
:

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}\cap\{r=r_{0}\}}\widehat{\psi}^{2}\,d\sigma\leq

\displaystyle\>C(1+\tau)^{-2+\max\{1-3\beta_{0},0\}+2\delta}\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{0},0,\delta}[\phi]\hskip 20.00003pt(\beta_{0}<1),

(10.29)

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}\cap\{r=r_{0}\}}\widehat{\psi}^{2}\,d\sigma\leq

\displaystyle\>C(1+\tau)^{-2-2n_{\beta}+\max\{2n_{\beta}+1-\beta_{1},0\}+2\delta}\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{0},0,\delta}[\phi]\hskip 20.00003pt(\beta_{0}>1).

(10.30)

Hence, by (
2.9
),

\displaystyle||\widehat{\psi}||_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma_{\tau})}\leq

\displaystyle\>C(1+\tau)^{-1+\frac{1}{2}\max\{1-3\beta_{0},0\}+\delta}\sqrt{\sum_{0\leq k\leq 2}\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{0},0,\delta}[\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{k}\phi]}\hskip 20.00003pt(\beta_{0}<1),

\displaystyle||\widehat{\psi}||_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma_{\tau})}\leq

\displaystyle\>C(1+\tau)^{-1-n_{\beta}+\frac{1}{2}\max\{2n_{\beta}+1-\beta_{1},0\}+\delta}\sqrt{\sum_{0\leq k\leq 2}\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{0},0,\delta}[\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{k}\phi]}\hskip 20.00003pt(\beta_{0}>1).

When
\beta_{0}>1
, we integrate
X(r^{1-\beta_{0}+\delta}\widehat{\phi}^{2})
from
r=\infty
and apply Proposition
10.8
to obtain moreover:

\begin{split}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}\cap\{r=r_{0}\}}r^{1-\beta_{0}+\delta}\widehat{\phi}^{2}\,d\sigma\leq&\>C\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}\cap\{r\geq r_{0}\}}r^{-\beta_{0}+\delta}\widehat{\phi}^{2}+r^{2-\beta_{0}}(X\widehat{\phi})^{2}\,d\sigma dr\\
\leq&\>C(1+\tau)^{-3-\beta_{0}-2n_{\beta}+\max\{2n_{\beta}+1-\beta_{1},0\}-2N+2\delta}\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{0},0,\delta}[\phi].\end{split}

Observe moreover that

\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}\cap\{r=r_{0}\}}r^{\delta}(w_{0}^{-1}\widehat{\phi})^{2}\,d\sigma\leq\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}\cap\{r=r_{0}\}}r^{1-\beta_{0}+\delta}\widehat{\phi}^{2}\,d\sigma,

so

||w_{0}^{-1}\widehat{\phi}||_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma_{\tau})}\leq C(1+\tau)^{-\frac{3}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{0}-n_{\beta}+\frac{1}{2}\max\{2n_{\beta}+1-\beta_{1},0\}+\delta}\sqrt{\sum_{0\leq k\leq 2}\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{0},0}[\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{k}\phi]}.

We similarly estimate when
\beta_{0}<1
, using Proposition
10.8
:

\begin{split}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}\cap\{r=r_{0}\}}r^{1-\beta_{0}+\delta}\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1}^{2}\,d\sigma\leq&\>C\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}\cap\{r\geq r_{0}\}}r^{-\beta_{0}+\delta}\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1}^{2}+r^{2-\beta_{0}}(X\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1})^{2}\,d\sigma dr\\
\leq&\>C(1+\tau)^{-3-\beta_{0}+2\delta}\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{0},0}[\phi]\end{split}

so

||w_{0}^{-1}\widehat{\phi}_{\geq 1}||_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma_{\tau})}\leq C(1+\tau)^{-\frac{3}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{0}+\delta}\sqrt{\sum_{0\leq k\leq 2}\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{0},0}[\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{k}\phi]}.

When
\beta_{0}<1
, we moreover integrate
X(w_{0}^{-1}\widehat{\phi}_{0})
from
r=r_{0}
to
r=r_{0}+\tau
to estimate:

\begin{split}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}\cap\{r=r_{0}\}}w_{0}^{-2}\widehat{\phi}_{0}^{2}\,d\sigma\leq&\>C\left(\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}\cap\{r_{0}\leq r\leq r_{0}+\tau\}}X(w_{0}^{-1}\widehat{\phi}_{0})\,d\sigma dr\right)^{2}+C\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}\cap\{r=r_{0}+\tau\}}w_{0}^{-2}\widehat{\phi}_{0}^{2}\,d\sigma\\
\leq&\>C\int_{r_{0}}^{r_{0}+\tau}r^{1-2\beta_{0}}\,dr\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}\cap\{r_{0}\leq r\leq r_{0}+\tau\}}r^{-1+2\beta_{0}}(X(w_{0}^{-1}\widehat{\phi}_{0}))^{2}\,d\sigma dr\\
&+C(r_{0}+\tau)^{-\beta_{0}}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}\cap\{r=r_{0}+\tau\}}\widehat{\psi}_{0}^{2}\,d\sigma\\
\leq&\>C\tau^{2-2\beta_{0}}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}\cap\{r_{0}\leq r\leq r_{0}+\tau\}}r^{-1+2\beta_{0}}(X(w_{0}^{-1}\widehat{\phi}_{0}))^{2}\,d\sigma dr+C(r_{0}+\tau)^{-\beta_{0}}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}\cap\{r=r_{0}+\tau\}}\widehat{\psi}_{0}^{2}\,d\sigma.\end{split}

By Proposition
10.8
together with the above
L^{\infty}
estimates for
\widehat{\psi}
it then follows that:

\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}\cap\{r=r_{0}\}}w_{0}^{-2}\widehat{\phi}_{0}^{2}\,d\sigma\leq C(1+\tau)^{\max\{1-3\beta_{0},0\}-\beta_{0}-3+\delta}\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{0},0}[\phi],

so

||w_{0}^{-1}\widehat{\phi}_{0}||_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma_{\tau}\cap\{r\leq R+\tau\})}\leq C(1+\tau)^{\frac{1}{2}\max\{1-3\beta_{0},0\}-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{0}-\frac{3}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\delta}\sqrt{\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{0},0}[\phi]}.

To conclude that (
10.27
) holds, we combine the
L^{\infty}
estimates, replace
\phi
with
T^{N-k}\phi
, modifying the corresponding energy decay estimates appropriately with an additional factor
(1+\tau)^{-2N}
and observe that we can write:

\displaystyle-1+\frac{1}{2}\max\{1-3\beta_{0},0\}=

\displaystyle\>-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{0}-\frac{1}{2}\min\{2\beta_{0},1-\beta_{0}+n_{\beta_{0}},\beta_{1}-\beta_{0}\}\hskip 10.00002pt(\beta_{0}<1)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{0}-\frac{1}{2}\min\{1-\beta_{0}+2n_{\beta},\beta_{1}-\beta_{0}\}-=

\displaystyle\>-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{0}-\frac{1}{2}\min\{2\beta_{0},1-\beta_{0}+n_{\beta_{0}},\beta_{1}-\beta_{0}\}\hskip 10.00002pt(\beta_{0}>1).

∎

Corollary 10.11
.

The integrals

\begin{split}\phi^{(1+n)}(\tau,r,\theta,\varphi)=&(-1)^{1+n}\int_{\tau}^{\infty}\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\infty}\ldots\int_{\tau_{n}}^{\infty}\phi(s,r,\theta,\varphi)\,dsd\tau_{n}\ldots d\tau_{1}\end{split}

are well-defined for
n\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
with
0\leq n\leq n_{\beta}
and satisfy:

\displaystyle\phi^{(1+n)}(\tau,r,\theta,\varphi)=

\displaystyle\>\frac{1}{n!}\int_{\tau}^{\infty}(\tau-s)^{n}\phi(s,r,\theta,\varphi)\,ds,

(10.31)

\displaystyle\phi^{(1+n)}(\tau,r,\theta,\varphi)=

\displaystyle\>T^{-n}\phi(\tau,r,\theta,\varphi).

(10.32)

Proof.

The integrals
\phi^{(1+n)}(\tau,r,\theta,\varphi)
are well-defined for
0\leq n\leq n_{\beta}
by the decay-in-time properties in Proposition
10.10
. To arrive at (
10.31
), we integrate by parts multiple times, making use of the decay-in-time of
\phi
from Proposition
10.10
.

We moreover have that
T^{k}\phi^{(1+n_{\beta})}(\tau,r,\theta,\varphi)=\phi^{(1+n_{\beta}-k)}(\tau,r,\theta,\varphi)
for
0\leq k\leq 1+n_{\beta}
. In particular,

T^{1+n_{\beta}}\phi^{(1+n_{\beta})}=\phi.

By Proposition
9.9
and Corollary
9.10
, we also have that

T^{1+n_{\beta}}(T^{-1-n_{\beta}}\phi)=\phi.

Hence, we must have that there exists functions
P_{j}(r,\theta,\varphi)
, with
0\leq j\leq n_{\beta}
such that

T^{-1-n_{\beta}}\phi=\phi^{(1+n_{\beta})}+\sum_{j=0}^{n_{\beta}}\tau^{j}P_{j}(r,\theta,\varphi).

By construction,
\phi^{(1+n_{\beta})}(\tau,r,\theta,\varphi)\to 0
as
\tau\to\infty
. From Proposition
10.10
, it follows moreover that
T^{-1-n_{\beta}}\phi(\tau,r,\theta,\varphi)\to 0
as
\tau\to\infty
. Hence,
P_{j}\equiv 0
for all
0\leq j\leq n_{\beta}
and (
10.32
) holds.
∎

11
Late-time asymptotics for
\phi_{\geq\ell}

The results in §
9
and §
10
(for
\beta_{0}>1
, or equivalently,
\alpha>0
) can immediately be generalized to obtain late-time asymptotics and decay estimates in time for
\phi_{\geq\ell}
. This follows from the identities:

\displaystyle(-\not{\Delta}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}+V_{\alpha})\phi_{\ell}=

\displaystyle\>(\ell(\ell+1)+V_{\alpha})\phi_{\ell}=V_{\alpha+\ell(\ell+1)}\phi_{\ell},\hskip 10.00002pt\textnormal{with}

\displaystyle V_{\alpha+\ell(\ell+1)}=

\displaystyle\>V_{\alpha}+\ell(\ell+1)r^{-2},

\displaystyle\beta_{\ell;\alpha}:=

\displaystyle\>\sqrt{1+4\alpha+4\ell(\ell+1)}=\beta_{0;\alpha+\ell(\ell+1)},

\displaystyle\beta_{\ell+1;\alpha}\leq

\displaystyle\>\beta_{1;\alpha+\ell(\ell+1)}.

The above identities and inequality allow us to replace
w_{0}
with
w_{\ell}
,
\beta_{0}
with
\beta_{\ell}
and let
\beta_{\ell+1}
take on the role of
\beta_{1}
in the main estimates of §
9
and §
10
. In particular, this means we need to define
n_{\beta_{\ell}}=\lfloor\frac{\beta_{\ell}+1}{2}\rfloor
and:

\mathfrak{I}_{\beta_{\ell}}[\phi]:=\lim_{r\to\infty}r^{\frac{1}{2}+(\frac{1}{2}\beta_{\ell}-n_{\beta_{\ell}})}X(rT^{-1-n_{\beta_{\ell}}}\phi_{\ell})=-\frac{(1-\beta_{\ell})(3-\beta_{\ell})\ldots(2n_{\beta_{\ell}}+1-\beta_{\ell})}{2n_{\beta_{\ell}}!\beta_{\ell}(1-\beta_{\ell})\ldots(n_{\beta_{\ell}}-\beta_{\ell})}\int_{2M}^{\infty}w_{\ell}F_{\ell}[\phi_{\ell}]\,dr^{\prime}

and, furthermore,

\displaystyle\widehat{\phi}_{\geq\ell}=

\displaystyle\>\phi_{\geq\ell}-\Phi^{(\beta_{\ell})},

\displaystyle\Phi^{(\beta_{\ell})}(\tau,r,\theta,\varphi)=

\displaystyle\>\Phi_{\ell}(\theta,\varphi)w_{\ell}(r)(\tau+1+2r)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\beta_{\ell}}{2}}(\tau+1)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\beta_{\ell}}{2}},

\displaystyle\Phi_{\ell}(\theta,\varphi)=

\displaystyle\>\sum_{|m|\leq\ell}\Phi_{\ell m}Y_{\ell m}(\theta,\varphi),

\displaystyle\Phi_{\ell m}\in

\displaystyle\>\mathbb{R}.

Without loss of generality, we can fix
-\ell\leq m\leq\ell
and assume
\Phi_{\ell m}\in\mathbb{R}\setminus\{0\}
. Furthermore, we choose

\Phi_{\ell}=(-1)^{n_{\beta_{\ell}}}n_{\beta_{\ell}}!\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{\beta_{\ell}+1}{2}\right)\Gamma\left(\frac{\beta_{\ell}-1-2n_{\beta_{\ell}}}{2}\right)\cos\left(\frac{\beta_{\ell}-2n_{\beta_{\ell}}}{2\pi}\right)}{2^{n_{\beta_{\ell}}-\beta_{\ell}}\pi\Gamma(\beta-n_{\beta_{\ell}})}\mathfrak{I}_{\beta_{\ell}}[\phi].

We then conclude by repeating the proof of Proposition
10.10
(in the case
\beta_{0}>1
) with the above considerations in mind:

Proposition 11.1
.

Let
\beta_{\ell}\neq 2l+1
for all
l\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
, and let
\delta>0
be arbitrarily small. Then there exists a constant
C=C(M,h,V_{\alpha},N,\delta)>0
such that:

\displaystyle||T^{N}\phi-\Phi_{\ell}w_{\ell}T^{N}((1+\tau+2r)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{\ell}}(1+\tau)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{\ell}})||_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma_{\tau})}

(11.1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\>Cr^{-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\beta_{\ell}}(1+\tau+2r)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{\ell}}(1+\tau)^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{\ell}-\frac{1}{2}\min\{1-\beta_{\ell}+2n_{\beta_{\ell}},\beta_{\ell+1}-\beta_{\ell}\}-N+\delta}\sqrt{\sum_{0\leq k\leq 2}\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{\ell},N,\delta}[\not{\nabla}_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}^{k}\phi_{\geq\ell}]},

with
\mathbf{D}_{\beta_{\ell},N,\delta}
defined in §
4.1
.

Furthermore, we can repeat the proof of Corollary
10.11
to obtain:

Corollary 11.2
.

The integrals

\begin{split}\phi_{\geq\ell}^{(1+n)}(\tau,r,\theta,\varphi)=&(-1)^{1+n}\int_{\tau}^{\infty}\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\infty}\ldots\int_{\tau_{n}}^{\infty}\phi_{\geq\ell}(s,r,\theta,\varphi)\,dsd\tau_{n}\ldots d\tau_{1}\end{split}

are well-defined for
n\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
with
0\leq n\leq n_{\beta_{\ell}}
and satisfy:

\displaystyle\phi_{\geq\ell}^{(1+n)}(\tau,r,\theta,\varphi)=

\displaystyle\>\frac{1}{n!}\int_{\tau}^{\infty}(\tau-s)^{n}\phi_{\geq\ell}(s,r,\theta,\varphi)\,ds,

(11.2)

\displaystyle\phi_{\geq\ell}^{(1+n)}(\tau,r,\theta,\varphi)=

\displaystyle\>T^{-n}\phi_{\geq\ell}(\tau,r,\theta,\varphi).

(11.3)

Appendix A
Properties of the weight functions
w_{\ell}

We define the operator
\mathcal{L}_{w_{\ell}}
as follows:

\mathcal{L}_{w_{\ell}}u=\frac{d}{dr}\left(Dr^{2}\frac{du}{dr}\right)-[r^{2}V_{\alpha}+\ell(\ell+1)]u,

with
V_{\alpha}
satisfying Assumption (
A)
).

Let
R>2M
be arbitrarily large and let
H_{w_{\ell},p}^{N}
,
H_{w_{\ell},p;1}^{N}
denote the completions of the function spaces:

\{f\in C_{0}^{\infty}([2M,\infty)\,|\,f(R)=0\}

under the norm

\displaystyle||u||^{2}_{w_{\ell},N,p}:=

\displaystyle\>\sum_{k=0}^{N}\int_{2M}^{\infty}r^{p}\left(\left(r\frac{d}{dr}\right)^{k}u\right)^{2}\,dr,

(A.1)

\displaystyle||u||^{2}_{w_{\ell},N,p;1}:=

\displaystyle\>\sum_{k=0}^{N}\int_{2M}^{\infty}r^{p}\left(\left(r\frac{d}{dr}\right)^{k}u\right)^{2}\,dr+\int_{2M}^{\infty}Dr^{p}\left(\left(r\frac{d}{dr}\right)^{N+1}u\right)^{2}\,dr.

(A.2)

respectively, with
p\neq-1
and

\displaystyle-\beta_{\ell}<

\displaystyle\>p<\beta_{\ell}\hskip 10.00002pt\textnormal{if $\beta_{\ell}\leq 1$},

\displaystyle-\beta_{\ell}<

\displaystyle\>p<2-\beta_{\ell}\hskip 10.00002pt\textnormal{if $\beta_{\ell}>1$}.

Proposition A.1
.

Let
u\in C_{0}^{\infty}([2M,R)\cup(R,\infty))
, then, for sufficiently large
R>2M
, there exists a
C=C(\alpha,p,N,R)>0
, such that

||u||_{w_{\ell},N,p;1}\leq C||\mathcal{L}_{w_{\ell}}u||_{w_{\ell},N,p}.

Proof.

First, consider the case
N=0
. We first multiply
\mathcal{L}_{w_{\ell}}u
with
-u
and integrate on
[2M,R)
to obtain:

\begin{split}\int_{2M}^{R}&Dr^{2}\left(\frac{du}{dr}\right)^{2}+\left[r^{2}V_{\alpha}+\ell(\ell+1)\right]u^{2}\,dr\\
=&\>\int_{2M}^{R}u\mathcal{L}_{w_{\ell}}u\,dr.\end{split}

We apply Young’s inequality to estimate:

|u\mathcal{L}_{w_{\ell}}u|\leq\epsilon u^{2}+\frac{1}{4\epsilon}\left(\mathcal{L}_{w_{\ell}}u\right)^{2}.

Furthermore, by (
2.10
)

\int_{2M}^{R}u^{2}\,dr\leq 4\int_{2M}^{R}D^{2}r^{2}\left(\frac{du}{dr}\right)^{2}\,dr,

so, using that
\inf r^{2}V_{\alpha}>-\frac{1}{4}
, we obtain:

\begin{split}\int_{2M}^{R}&Dr^{2}\left(\frac{du}{dr}\right)^{2}+u^{2}\,dr\leq C(\alpha)\int_{2M}^{R}(\mathcal{L}_{w_{\ell}}u)^{2}\,dr.\end{split}

(A.3)

Then we multiply by
-r^{p}u
and integrate by parts on
[R,\infty)
(using that
u
vanishes at
r=R
) to obtain

\begin{split}\int_{R}^{\infty}&r^{2+p}\left(\frac{du}{dr}\right)^{2}+r^{p}\left[r^{2}V_{\alpha}+\ell(\ell+1)-\frac{1}{2}p(p+1)+O(r^{-1})\right]u^{2}\,dr\\
=&\>\int_{R}^{\infty}r^{p}u\mathcal{L}_{w_{\ell}}u\,dr.\end{split}

(A.4)

Furthermore, by the fact that
p\neq-1
, we can apply (
2.10
) to obtain:

\int_{R}^{\infty}r^{p}u^{2}\,dr\leq 4(p+1)^{-2}\int_{R}^{\infty}r^{p+2}\left(\frac{du}{dr}\right)^{2}\,dr.

Since
r^{2}V_{\alpha}=\alpha+O_{\infty}(r^{-1})
, we therefore need additionally that:

\alpha+\ell(\ell+1)-\frac{1}{2}p(p+1)>-\frac{1}{4}(p+1)^{2},

or
-\beta_{\ell}<p<\beta_{\ell}
, and
R\gg M
suitably large to absorb the
u^{2}
term on the left-hand side of (
A.4
), to obtain

\begin{split}\int_{R}^{\infty}&r^{2+p}\left(\frac{du}{dr}\right)^{2}+r^{p}u^{2}\,dr\leq C(\alpha,p)\int_{R}^{\infty}r^{p}(\mathcal{L}_{w_{\ell}}u)^{2}\,dr.\end{split}

(A.5)

We combine (
A.3
) and (
A.5
) to obtain (
A.1
) with
N=0
. The case
N>0
then follows by induction, after integrating
-r^{p}(r\frac{d}{dr})^{k}(u)(r\frac{d}{dr})^{k}(\mathcal{L}_{w_{\ell}}u)
.
∎

Corollary A.2
.

Let
N\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
and
\delta>0
suitably small. Suppose
f\in H_{w_{\ell},p}^{N}
with
p=\min\{\beta_{\ell},2-\beta_{\ell}\}-\delta
and
p\neq-1
. Then there exists a unique
u\in H_{w_{\ell},p;1}^{N}
such that
\mathcal{L}_{w_{\ell}}u=f
. In particular, if
f\in\cap_{k\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}H_{w_{\ell},p}^{k}
, then:

\displaystyle u\in

\displaystyle\>C^{\infty}([0,\infty)),

\displaystyle u(r)=

\displaystyle\>O_{\infty}(r^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\beta_{\ell}+\delta})\hskip 10.00002pt\alpha+\ell(\ell+1)\leq 0,

\displaystyle u(r)=

\displaystyle\>O_{\infty}(r^{-\frac{3}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\beta_{\ell}+\delta})\hskip 10.00002pt\alpha+\ell(\ell+1)\geq 0.

Proof.

We apply Lax–Milgram with the bilinear map
B:H_{w_{\ell},p;1}^{0}\times H_{w_{\ell},p;1}^{0}\to\mathbb{R}
, defined by:

\begin{split}B(u,v)=&\>\int_{2M}^{R}Dr^{2}\frac{du}{dr}(r)\frac{dv}{dr}(r)+[r^{2}V_{\alpha}+\ell(\ell+1)]u(r)v(r)\,dr\\
&+\int_{R}^{\infty}Dr^{2+p}\frac{du}{dr}(r)\frac{dv}{dr}(r)+r^{2+p}\left[r^{2}V_{\alpha}+\ell(\ell+1)-\frac{1}{2}p(p+1)\right]u(r)v(r)\,dr,\end{split}

to obtain existence of solutions in
H_{w_{\ell},p}^{0}
, where the necessary coercivity properties of
B
and the higher regularity properties of the solution follow from Proposition
A.1
, together with standard interior regularity estimates.

Smoothness of
u
follows from standard Sobolev estimates and the decay in
r
follows by applying the fundamental theorem of calculus:

\begin{split}(r^{\frac{p}{2}+\frac{1}{2}}u)^{2}(r)=&\>(r^{\frac{p}{2}+\frac{1}{2}}u)^{2}(r_{0})+\int_{r_{0}}^{r}(\frac{p}{2}+\frac{1}{2})r^{p}u^{2}+2r^{p+1}uu^{\prime}\,dr\\
\lesssim(r^{\frac{p}{2}+\frac{1}{2}}u)^{2}(r_{0})+\int_{r_{0}}^{r}r^{p}u^{2}+r^{p+2}(u^{\prime})^{2}\,dr,\end{split}

together with Proposition
A.1
, and analogous estimates for
u
replaced by
(r\frac{d}{dr})^{k}u
, with
p=\beta_{\ell}-\delta
if
\beta_{\ell}\leq 1
and
p=2-\beta-\delta
if
\beta_{\ell}>1
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
3.3
.

Define
v_{\ell}=r^{-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\beta_{\ell}}+b_{0}r^{-\frac{3}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\beta_{\ell}}
and denote

f_{\ell}(r)=(Dr^{2}v_{\ell}^{\prime})^{\prime}(r)-(r^{2}V_{\alpha}+\ell(\ell+1))v_{\ell}(r).

Choose
b_{0}
such that
f(R)=0
. Note that
f
is smooth on
[0,\infty)
, and moreover
f_{\ell}=O_{\infty}(r^{-\frac{3}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\beta_{\ell}})
. Hence,
f\in H^{N}_{w_{\ell},p}
for all
N\in\mathbb{N}
with
p=\min\{\beta_{\ell},2-\beta_{\ell}\}-\delta
. We apply Corollary
A.2
to conclude that there exists a unique function
u\in C^{\infty}([0,\infty))
such that
\mathcal{L}_{w_{\ell}}u=f_{\ell}
and the properties listed in Corollary
A.2
hold.

Finally, we define
w_{\ell}=u-v_{\ell}
to conclude that
\mathcal{L}_{w_{\ell}}w_{\ell}=0
and
w_{\ell}
satisfies the asymptotic properties stated in Lemma
3.3
. Note that strict positivity of
w_{\ell}
can be shown to hold via a contradiction argument: suppose there exists
r_{0}\geq 2M
such that
w_{0}(r_{0})=0
. Then we can apply the estimates from the proof of Proposition
A.1
with
R
replaced by
r_{0}
to obtain
L^{2}
-bounds for
w_{\ell}
in
[r_{0},\infty)
with
p=0
, from which it follows that
w_{\ell}\equiv 0
in
[r_{0},\infty)
, which is a contradiction with the asymptotic properties of
w_{\ell}(r)
as
r\to\infty
.
∎

Remark A.1
.

From Proposition
A.2
, it follows that Assumption
A)
implies Assumption
A’)
.
